- **requirements-standards**: **Pragmatic Institute Framework** (High relevance) distinguishes MRD (problem space: personas, their problems, market opportunity) from PRD (solution space: features, user stories, acceptance criteria). Its key insight: requirements should be written as **problems to be solved for specific personas**, not feature lists. This prevents over-engineering and keeps focus on product-market fit. ([aRustyDev/agents](https://github.com/aRustyDev/agents/tree/HEAD/.claude/plans/plugins/planning-workflow/scaffold/skills/requirements-standards) · ★8 · 2 copies)
